## 🔧 Fix Commands

To fix a specific line:
```bash
# sed -i "LINENUMBERs/^/\/\/ /" FILENAME
# Example: sed -i "10s/^/\/\/ /" src/utils/YourClass.ts
```

To fix all TS1434 errors automatically (use with caution):
```bash
npx tsc --noEmit --skipLibCheck 2>&1 | grep "TS1434" | grep -v ".test\|.spec" | \
while read line; do 
  file=$(echo $line | cut -d'(' -f1); 
  num=$(echo $line | sed 's/.*(\([0-9]*\),.*/\1/'); 
  sed -i "${num}s/^/\/\/ /" "$file"; 
done
```
